    25 | 400 | 480 | 10,900 | 4,750 | 480,000 | 8d | 12
  * The Dragon Duke is the second aerial Hero and the sixth overall in terms of release who attacks all targets from melee range, introduced on March 1, 2026. He is unlocked by upgrading the Hero Hall to level 9, which requires Town Hall 15.
  * Like the Minion Prince , the Dragon Duke will defend the village when assigned to a Hero Banner . The Dragon Duke only defends his territory around his banner and will retreat if lured too far out. He does not need to be defeated for an attack to end in a 3-star. When defending, the Dragon Duke will fly, making him unreachable by units that only target ground such as the Barbarian King . He can neither use his passive ability nor use his Hero Equipment during this time.
  * When defending, the Dragon Duke will fly, making him unreachable by units that only target ground such as the Barbarian King . He can neither use his passive ability nor use his Hero Equipment during this time.
  * When unlocked, the Dragon Duke starts with the Fire Heart and Flame Blower. The Fire Heart allows the Dragon Duke to create a large death explosion. It gives a passive increase to damage and max health, and grants him self healing. The Flame Blower creates a cone of fire forward from the Dragon Duke and deals significant damage, similar to the Baby Dragon in the Builder Base, and grants an increased health recovery. It gives a passive increase to maximum health.
  * The Fire Heart allows the Dragon Duke to create a large death explosion. It gives a passive increase to damage and max health, and grants him self healing.
  * The Flame Blower creates a cone of fire forward from the Dragon Duke and deals significant damage, similar to the Baby Dragon in the Builder Base, and grants an increased health recovery. It gives a passive increase to maximum health.
  * Upgrading the Blacksmith to level 9 and 10 unlocks the Stun Blaster and Electro Fangs, respectively. The Stun Blaster creates a stunning shockwave around the Dragon Duke that stuns and damages buildings and defending troops except resource buildings and Walls , and grants an increased health recovery. It gives a passive increase to damage and maximumm health. The Electro Fangs allows the Dragon Duke's regular attacks to gain the chain lightning effect, jumping to additional targets beyond the Duke's original target. It gives a passive increase to maximum health.
  * The Stun Blaster creates a stunning shockwave around the Dragon Duke that stuns and damages buildings and defending troops except resource buildings and Walls , and grants an increased health recovery. It gives a passive increase to damage and maximumm health.
  * The Electro Fangs allows the Dragon Duke's regular attacks to gain the chain lightning effect, jumping to additional targets beyond the Duke's original target. It gives a passive increase to maximum health.
  * This ability is not disabled by the Dragon Duke's Pet if it is flying, like the Angry Jelly , but flying Pets from other Heroes will disable this ability. Summoned units from Pets (like Boogers from Sneezy ) can disable this ability. If the Dragon Duke is defeated while his Pet survives, that Pet will no longer be considered his own for this particular interaction; if the Dragon Duke is revived later by a Revive Spell , his Pet can disable the ability. The Phoenix does not disable the Royal Rampage ability when she hatches and revives the Dragon Duke, but only while her ability is active.
  * Summoned units from Pets (like Boogers from Sneezy ) can disable this ability.
  * When alone, the Dragon Duke can prove to be a very powerful Hero, able to destroy a compartment by himself with the help of his Flame Blower or Stun Blaster and Fire Heart , depending on the strategy. He is also resistant to trap damage, allowing him to endure Seeking Air Mines with ease. Be careful, as a single air unit (like Minions and Balloons ) can disable this ability, so keep in mind.
  * Other aerial Pets can be used, like the Greedy Raven and the Electro Owl , but the former only targets resource buildings while the latter cannot keep up with the Dragon Duke.
  * Be mindful of the defending Dragon Duke, as he is untargetable by units which target ground. Use Dragons, Baby Dragons, the Archer Queen , the Royal Champion , or other units that target air to knock him out of battle, owing to his extremely high hitpoints.
  * As the Dragon Duke is one of the Heroes with the highest hitpoints, using Revive Spells when he is knocked out can be extremely powerful as they allow him to continually wreak havoc onto the base, increasing the chances of a successful attack.
  * He can become overwhelmed by swarms like Skeletons from Witches and Skeleton Traps , as he attacks one target at a time, even when using his fiery breath. However, this does not happen if he still has his Ability. The Flame Blower is able to take out all the Skeletons in front of him. The Stun Blaster deals enough damage to take out the Skeletons in a huge radius. And the Rocket Backpack not only takes out the Skeletons, but also causes the Dragon Duke to fly to the other side of the map.
  * However, this does not happen if he still has his Ability. The Flame Blower is able to take out all the Skeletons in front of him. The Stun Blaster deals enough damage to take out the Skeletons in a huge radius. And the Rocket Backpack not only takes out the Skeletons, but also causes the Dragon Duke to fly to the other side of the map.
  * Aerial traps like Seeking Air Mines are not as effective when the Dragon Duke is alone. However, if the attacker accidentally places an aerial unit near him, this disables his Royal Rampage ability, allowing the Seeking Air Mines to significantly damage him if not knock him out of battle.


# Spells

## Lightning Spell  (rev 588160)
- info: Damage Radius: 2 tiles; Stun Time: 0.1s; Housing Space: 1; Spell Factory Level Required: 1
- info: Radius: 1.5 tiles; Stun Duration: 0.5 seconds; Housing Space: 1; Required Spell Factory: Lightning Spell Factory
- stats: Level | Damage | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    10 | 600 | 10,000,000 | 6d | 13
    11 | 640 | 13,500,000 | 7d | 14
    12 | 680 | 18,500,000 | 10d | 15
    13 | 720 | 27,000,000 | 14d | 16
- stats: Level | Damage
    2 | 325
    3 | 375
    4 | 425
    5 | 475
  * The Lightning Spell is the first Elixir spell unlocked in the Spell Factory , and is automatically unlocked once the Spell Factory has finished being built, requiring the Town Hall to be at level 5. The Lightning Spell does damage to Buildings and enemy Troops in a small radius, with the exception of resource storages, the Town Hall , and the Clan Castle . If a Defensive Building or troop is struck by the bolt, it is stunned briefly and retargets.
  * When combined with an Earthquake Spell or two, Lightning Spells can be used in large numbers to destroy high hitpoint buildings such as the Eagle Artillery , Monolith , Firespitter , or a Scattershot directly; however, this is often not worth it unless you can destroy at least two such buildings, or otherwise get value (such as by defeating the enemy Archer Queen at the same time).
  * A common use is to use lure Clan Castle troops near defenses and then drop a Lightning Spell to eliminate the troops if their health is low enough and simultaneously damage the defenses in its radius. Lightning Spells can be used to kill/damage Heroes directly too. However, if one simply wants to take out enemy troops, Poison Spell does better since it does more damage and slows them. The main downside is that Poison does not affect structures.
  * However, if one simply wants to take out enemy troops, Poison Spell does better since it does more damage and slows them. The main downside is that Poison does not affect structures.
  * Only unweaponized (level 1) Builder's Huts can be destroyed by a single Lightning Spell; if the Builder's Hut is weaponized (upgraded to level 2 or higher), at least two or three Lightning Spells will be needed to destroy it.
  * Some farming strategies such as Barch or Super Barbarians often do not need spell support. Lightning Spells are useful to fill up the spell space, so that resource collectors that are out of reach of these strategies can be damaged and looted, or to achieve 50% as described above. Note that using the Lightning Spell on the Town Hall, Clan Castle or resource storages does no damage to them.
  * However, beyond low-level gameplay, Walls often have too much hitpoints for this to be practical. Earthquake Spells and Jump Spells can be used instead.
  * When trying to damage multiple buildings at once with a Lightning Spell, there are several ways to visualize its area of effect. One easy visualization is as follows: if dropped at the center of a tile, it will affect exactly 21 tiles - a 5x5 area (centered on the tile where the Lightning Spell is dropped) with the four corners removed.
  * At Town Hall 8, a level 6 Air Defense can be destroyed with 4 level 5 Lightning Spells if done properly, thus making room for an extra Rage Spell that will dramatically improve mass Dragon attacks. If you have four level 4 Lightning Spells and can get an Earthquake Spell (either brewed or donated), that will also destroy a level 6 Air Defense.
  * If you have four level 4 Lightning Spells and can get an Earthquake Spell (either brewed or donated), that will also destroy a level 6 Air Defense.
  * At Town Hall 10, new Town Hall 10 players have a great disadvantage against other TH10s in mass Dragon attacks as they have no Inferno Towers and a level 7 Air Defense can be destroyed easily by 3 level 7 Lightning Spells. 11+1 Lightning Spells can take out all of the enemy's Air Defenses and depending on layout both X-Bows, rendering the enemy nearly defenseless (with only the Archer Queen, Archer Towers , Wizard Towers, Air Sweepers and possibly Clan Castle troops able to defend).
  * Both the Lightning and Freeze Spells can disrupt the damage of a single-target Inferno Tower since it stuns. Lightning Spells are better used when Inferno Tower has low health, as the Inferno Tower is only left inactive for a short amount of time. Similarly, it can also cause the Eagle Artillery to start its wind-up once again.
  * Separate your Air Defenses from your Air Sweepers . Letting attackers destroy both your Air Defenses and at least one Air Sweeper is a death sentence from Mass Dragons, Hydra or Lavaloon attacks, as the Air Sweeper is also key in defending against them. Once the Eagle Artillery is merged with the Town Hall, the Eagle Artillery within the Inferno Artillery becomes immune to Lightning Spells.
  * Once the Eagle Artillery is merged with the Town Hall, the Eagle Artillery within the Inferno Artillery becomes immune to Lightning Spells.

## Healing Spell  (rev 588153)
- info: Radius: 5 tiles; Number of Pulses: 41; Time Between Pulses: 0.3s; Housing Space: 2; Spell Factory Level Required: 2
- info: Radius: 4.5 tiles; Duration: 2 attacks; Time Between Pulses: 0.3s; Housing Space: 3; Required Spell Factory: Heal Spell Factory
- stats: Level | Total Healing | Healing per Pulse | Total Healing on Heroes | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    9 | 2,255 | 55 | 1,240.25 | 11,000,000 | 6d 12h | 13
    10 | 2,460 | 60 | 1,353 | 14,000,000 | 7d | 14
    11 | 2,747 | 67 | 1,510.85 | 19,000,000 | 10d 12h | 15
    12 | 3,075 | 75 | 1,691.25 | 29,000,000 | 15d | 16
- stats: Level | Healing per Second | Healing per Pulse
    2 | 93 | 28
    3 | 106 | 32
    4 | 120 | 36
    5 | 133 | 40
  * "Heal your Troops to keep them in the fight! Cast this spell to create a Ring of Healing. Your units will be healed while they are inside this ring."
  * The Healing Spell is the second spell unlocked at Level 2 Spell Factory , requiring the Town Hall to be at level 6.
  * The Healing Spell appears to be a bottle filled with light yellow liquid that heals units. When placed, the spell bottle falls to the ground and forms a stationary ring of healing on the ground. All friendly Troops and Heroes within the ring will be healed ( ground or air ), including Healers and Druids .
  * While Heroes can also be healed by the Healing Spell, the rate of healing is reduced to 55% the rate of normal troops. On the other hand, Siege Machines are completely unaffected by the Healing Spell.
  * The Healing Spell lasts for a total of 12 seconds (40 healing 'pulses' every 0.3 seconds).
  * Visually, the Healing Spell provides a medium-ranged radius with yellow shades of glowing color representing the healing effect. A similar effect can be seen while the Healer or Druid is healing.
  * It is generally better to use the Healing Spell when a large number of your troops are together, as each troop will be healed individually at the same rate (irrespective of how many troops are being healed).
  * Giants , Hog Riders , Miners and Valkyries all benefit significantly from a Healing Spell, as they have moderate health and can get healed quickly.
  * It is best to save your Healing Spells for when your troops are significantly damaged (when many of them are around half health) to maximize the spell's potential.
  * It can also be used for healing units such as Goblins while they attack Gold Storages and Elixir Storages . However, this might not be effective, as a Mortar or Wizard Tower of sufficient level may be able to instantly defeat them.
  * Any unit that moves out of the Healing Spell's radius will immediately stop being healed. As such, before you place a Healing Spell, make sure that you predict where your units are going to go, so they stay inside the radius longer.
  * The Healing Spell goes well with the Rage Spell , due to the fact that the Rage Spell makes troops faster and stronger while healing them makes them much more durable as well. However, the Rage Spell's speed boost may make troops leave the healing circle faster, so should be used sparingly (such as when tackling the core of a base). It is very effective when they are used with high-health and/or slow troops that are not in range with many defenses.
  * It is very effective when they are used with high-health and/or slow troops that are not in range with many defenses.
  * You can stack Healing Spells on top of each other to increase the effect.
  * Defending heals is all about where the heals will be placed and what to do to counter them.
  * Keeping splash damage far apart can minimize the value of the Healing Spell. Additionally, keep Giant Bombs away from splash defenses, so they do not get healed over.
  * Bomb Towers , with their considerable damage upon destruction, can easily force Healing Spells similarly to Giant Bombs.
  * Spring Traps should be used where you expect heals, as then troops will be at their strongest so this will remove them when they are at their strongest.

## Rage Spell  (rev 587899)
- info: Radius: 5 tiles; Number of Pulses: 60; Time Between Pulses: 0.3s; Boost Time: 1s; Housing Space: 2; Spell Factory Level Required: 3
- info: Radius: 4.5 tiles; Duration: 2 attacks; Housing Space: 3; Required Spell Factory: Rage Spell Factory
- stats: Level | Damage Increase | Speed Increase | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    4 | 160% | 26 | 1,000,000 | 1d | 5
    5 | 170% | 28 | 2,000,000 | 2d | 6
    6 | 180% | 30 | 5,000,000 | 4d | 10
    7 | 190% | 32 | 26,000,000 | 14d | 16
- stats: Level | Damage Increase | Speed Increase
    2 | 80% | 12
    3 | 90% | 14
    4 | 100% | 16
    5 | 110% | 18
  * The Rage Spell is the third spell unlocked when the Spell Factory is level 3 or above, requiring the Town Hall to be at least level 7. It is a large bottle filled with purple liquid that creates a translucent purple ring on the battlefield that boosts the movement speed and damage of any friendly Troops and Heroes inside the ring when dropped in an area. In the case of Healers or Druids , the Rage Spell increases their healing by a similar amount.
  * The Rage Spell's effect lasts for 18 seconds, which the effect will then dissipate afterwards.
  * The Rage Spell has a reduced effect on Heroes, only granting 50% of its damage and speed boosts to them. Siege Machines cannot be affected by the Rage Spell. Despite being an immovable troop, the Furnace can be enraged, despite that it does not attack. This also means that its summoned Firemites will not be affected.
  * Despite being an immovable troop, the Furnace can be enraged, despite that it does not attack. This also means that its summoned Firemites will not be affected.
  * There has been a great deal of confusion regarding whether the 'damage increase' listed is additive (+130% damage) or multiplicative (damage * 1.3). After multiple independent tests, it has been confirmed that the increase is indeed additive, meaning that troops under the influence of a level 1 Rage Spell do 130% more damage than those that are not; i.e. their damage (D) is calculated as follows:
  * The Rage Spell increases each individual unit 's damage by a fixed percentage (which increases as the spell upgrades). If any of your Troops or Heroes need more damage, drop a Rage Spell on them. Rage Spells make the troops and heroes stronger, so any buildings will be destroyed quicker than normal. Because the Rage Spell's damage increase depends on the troop's own damage, the Rage Spell is substantially less effective when placed on low damage troops such as Archers , as they do not do much damage as compared to troops with higher damage such as Wizards .
  * Because the Rage Spell's damage increase depends on the troop's own damage, the Rage Spell is substantially less effective when placed on low damage troops such as Archers , as they do not do much damage as compared to troops with higher damage such as Wizards .
  * In addition to increasing damage, the Rage Spell also carries a speed increase, which is very useful for slower troops such as P.E.K.K.As and Balloons . as they normally move quite slowly. Unlike the damage increase, the speed increase is a fixed value, so troops with lower movement speed will gain a substantial benefit. Faster troops like Hog Riders benefit less from this speed boost than slower troops like P.E.K.K.As.
  * One thing to note is that the Rage Spell does not increase the attack speed of troops. This means using a Rage Spell on slow-attacking troops such as a P.E.K.K.A, when trying to destroy massed amounts of defending troops , will be futile.
  * Druids can also do the same, but with higher healing, and are best used to heal the main army when they reach high-damaging areas of the base, but is however limited to the amount of targets.
  * The speed boost of the Rage Spell can be further useful in some cases to move your troops away from the explosion of the Town Hall quickly. This can be particularly useful with the Town Halls 13-16 Giga Inferno death explosions, which can leave behind detrimental slowing effects when detonated.
  * The most effective placement of the Rage Spell is to position it approximately 3-4 tiles toward where your troops' next target will be, this will give your Troops the maximum amount of time inside the Rage Spell's area of effect as they destroy their current target and move to the next one. If you drop it directly under their feet, they will quickly move out of its range once they have finished with their current target.
  * The Rage Spell goes well with the Healing Spell, due to the fact that the Rage Spell makes troops faster and stronger while healing them makes them much more durable as well. However, the Rage Spell's speed boost may make troops leave the healing circle faster, so they should be used sparingly (such as when tackling the core of a base, dealing against high-hitpoint buildings, and/or when breaking a ground attack out of a compartment). If you are planning to use both spells at the same time, position your Healing Spell in front of your Rage Spell.
  * The Rage Spell's effects do not stack, even if multiple Rage Spells are used on the same location; using two or more Rage Spells do not increase damage of the troops and heroes to a greater degree compared to using just one. However, if Rage Spells of different levels are used (which is possible through Clan Castle reinforcements), the highest-level Rage Spell will take effect.
  * Note that passive DPS from Hero Equipment and damage penalties from difficulty modifiers are applied before the Rage Spell modifies the Hero's damage.
  * Include buildings of lesser value near more important ones to distract raged troops. Retargeting speed does not increase with the Rage Spell.
  * Even if the Rage Spells are used to counter them, surround important defenses with trash and high HP buildings, in order to delay them. Even if they are all non-defensive buildings, troops still take quite a while to retarget and start up their attack.

## Jump Spell  (rev 588156)
- info: Radius: 3.5 tiles; Boost Time: 1s; Housing Space: 2; Spell Factory Level Required: 4
- info: Duration: 2 attacks; Housing Space: 1; Required Spell Factory: Jump Spell Factory
- stats: Level | Spell Duration | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    2 | 40 seconds | 1,000,000 | 1d | 5
    3 | 60 seconds | 2,000,000 | 2d | 8
    4 | 80 seconds | 5,000,000 | 4d | 11
    5 | 100 seconds | 8,000,000 | 5d | 13
- stats: Level | Spell Radius
    2 | 3.4 tiles
    3 | 3.6 tiles
    4 | 3.8 tiles
    5 | 4 tiles
  * " Walls slowing you down? Try making a shortcut! Cast this spell near enemy walls to create a route straight over them. Your troops will jump over affected walls pieces as if they weren't even there!"
  * The Jump Spell is the 4th Elixir Spell unlocked at level 4 Spell Factory along with the Freeze Spell , that requires the Town Hall to be at level 9. The Jump Spell allows ground Troops and Heroes to jump over Walls . Jump Spells have no effect whatsoever on units that can bypass Walls, such as air units , Hog Riders , Miners , Headhunters , the Grand Warden and the Royal Champion . The Jump Spell also will not affect the behavior of ground Siege Machines like the Wall Wrecker .
  * Jump Spells have no effect whatsoever on units that can bypass Walls, such as air units , Hog Riders , Miners , Headhunters , the Grand Warden and the Royal Champion . The Jump Spell also will not affect the behavior of ground Siege Machines like the Wall Wrecker .
  * Upgrading this spell will increase its duration on the battlefield by approximately 20 seconds (the level 3 Jump Spell lasts three times as long as the level 1 Jump Spell).
  * This spell is more effective on doubled or tripled Walls ; otherwise, the spell is not utilized to its maximum capability, making it much less valuable to use.
  * Combined with Wall Breakers , Jump Spells are quite useful for getting Troops deep into the interior of a base. Once Wall Breakers have initially breached the exterior Walls, any new ones tend to be attracted to adjacent compartments rather than those behind the breach. A Jump Spell, however, can be placed behind the initial breach to quickly allow Ground Troops access to the second layer of the base where the storages and heavy defenses ( Mortars , X-Bows , etc.) are typically positioned.
  * At Town Halls 9 and above, using Jump Spells are often a better option than using four Earthquake Spells (since they take up a lot of housing space). Although they do not destroy Walls, Jump Spells almost always last much longer than needed. Additionally, the Jump Spell requires half the housing space of four Earthquake Spells, and the Earthquake Spell radius is insignificantly larger than that of a Jump Spell. However, Earthquake Spells have the advantage of being able to damage most buildings, severely weakening high-hitpoint buildings such as the Town Hall , storages , or Eagle Artillery .
  * In order to defend against Jump Spells, make distracting compartments that are longer than a Jump Spell one way (Preferably to something important) and shorter than a Jump Spell another way to make troops go around whatever the compartment is protecting.
  * At Town Hall 9, use Traps to delay or defend possible places where a Jump Spell might be used long enough that it runs out before most troops make it through. However, at level 3 and 4, this becomes near impossible because of the extreme duration.
  * You could separate Wall segments so that the Jump Spell's radius will not cover both of them, which could reduce drastically its effectiveness.
  * Making a Jump Spell access too many compartments can split the army and will easily destroy their attack.

## Freeze Spell  (rev 622839)
- info: Radius: 3.5 tiles; Housing Space: 1; Spell Factory Level Required: 4
- stats: Level | Freeze Time | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    5 | 4.5 seconds | 4,200,000 | 2d 12h | 8
    6 | 5.0 seconds | 6,000,000 | 3d 12h | 9
    7 | 5.5 seconds | 7,000,000 | 5d | 10
    8 | 6.0 seconds | 28,000,000 | 15d 12h | 16
  * "When the going gets hot, stay frosty! The Freeze Spell sends out a cryogenic blast that temporarily immobilizes enemy troops and disables defensive buildings within its radius."
  * The Freeze Spell is the 5th Elixir Spell unlocked at level 4 Spell Factory alongside the Jump Spell , which requires the Town Hall to be at level 9. The Freeze Spell is used to temporarily disable defenses and/or ground and air troops from the enemy Clan Castle within a small radius. Until the spell's duration finishes, the affected defenses and/or enemy units will neither move nor attack (or heal, in the case of a Healer being frozen).
  * Enemy troops that are not within its radius when the spell is cast are unaffected by the Freeze Spell, even if they move into the area of effect while it is still active.
  * The Freeze Spell cannot freeze an enemy Hidden Tesla before it has been revealed. However, if the Hidden Tesla is revealed while the Freeze Spell is active, and the Hidden Tesla is in the radius of the spell, the Hidden Tesla will be inactive for the duration of the spell.
  * This spell can be used to freeze the Wizard Tower to allow your swarm of Barbarians or Archers to destroy it without being completely eliminated.
  * It can also freeze an Air Defense , which will buy some time for your air units to get closer to the Air Defense to destroy it. This can be extremely effective when utilizing an all- Balloon or an air army.
  * The Freeze Spell can also be used to freeze X-Bows , Inferno Towers and the Eagle Artillery , allowing stronger units such as P.E.K.K.As to live longer without single-target Inferno Towers, weaker units such as Barbarians to live longer without X-Bows and multi-target Inferno Towers, and clustered troops to live longer without the Eagle Artillery.
  * The Freeze Spell can also disable Town Hall weapons for a brief period, allowing the army to quickly destroy the Town Hall before it deals too much damage. This is especially useful in the case of the Inferno Artillery , as the splash damage will normally be able to wipe out Super Archers and Super Wizards that come out of the Battle Blimp .
  * This is especially useful in the case of the Inferno Artillery , as the splash damage will normally be able to wipe out Super Archers and Super Wizards that come out of the Battle Blimp .
  * In the case of the single-target Inferno Tower, the Freeze Spell is a much more effective option than the Healing Spell , as the Freeze Spell can completely negate the Inferno Tower for its entire duration, and once the spell's effect ends, the Inferno Tower's damage is reset to its initial state. Since a Freeze Spell now takes one spell space, you can now reset single-target Inferno Towers twice with two spell spaces; formerly you could only reset it once.
  * It can also freeze powerful Clan Castle troops such as Dragons and Wizards , buying your troops time to kill these units before they have a chance to wreak havoc on your attack plans. If cast in the correct location, it can also serve a double purpose by also freezing powerful defenses (such as Inferno Towers) at the same time. However, for weaker units, a Lightning Spell or Poison Spell may be a more expedient (and cheaper) option.
  * When most or all of your heavier units are destroyed, cast a Freeze Spell at area splash structures near the main part of your remaining army. This way, your Troops have a better chance at survival and may be able to take out the structure quickly before it becomes active again.
  * Both the Lightning and Freeze Spells can disrupt the damage of a single-target Inferno Tower. Freeze Spells are better used when an Inferno Tower has high health as the Inferno Tower is left inactive for a longer amount of time (and a short stun may not be as beneficial).
  * Freeze Spells are commonly used to defend troops from Inferno Towers . Using an Inferno Island (an Inferno Tower surrounded by Walls and a 2 or 3 tile gap with nothing else) will prevent extra value from the freeze. Similar islands can also be constructed with Scattershots or X-Bows .
  * Forcing too many Freeze Spells can ruin an attacker's plan. Spread out your splash defenses and make sure their range does not go outside Walls (or else a Giant can be placed and save a Freeze) to make many Freeze Spells required.
  * Hidden Teslas and Tornado Traps can delay attacking troops, making a Freeze Spell run out before the Freeze Spell's objective is destroyed. Use this to your advantage.

## Clone Spell  (rev 622840)
- info: Radius: 3.5 tiles; Spell Duration: 18s; Cloned Lifespan: 30s; Housing Space: 3; Spell Factory Level Required: 5
- stats: Level | Cloned Capacity | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    6 | 34 | 5,000,000 | 4d | 11
    7 | 38 | 8,000,000 | 5d | 12
    8 | 42 | 9,000,000 | 7d | 13
    9 | 48 | 26,000,000 | 13d 12h | 16
  * "Turn this Spell into a pop-up army ! Clone Spells create a circle of spawning that creates limited-lifetime copies of troops that enter it. It will only spawn up to a maximum housing space of troops."
  * The Clone Spell is the 6th Elixir Spell unlocked at level 5 Spell Factory , requiring the Town Hall to be at level 10. As the name of this spell implies, it clones troops that enter the spell's radius.
  * The copied troops are equal in level to the original troop, and appear with full health. However, they only last a limited amount of time, and can be destroyed prematurely by defenses .
  * It can also clone sub troops as well as troops spawned by Hero Equipment . Each subtroop has its own housing space value for this purpose. For example, each Skeleton (no matter how it is spawned), Bat or Frostmite has a housing space of 1, each Lava Pup , Ice Pup and Yetimite has a housing space of 2, each Golemite has 6, and each Big Boy has 10.
  * Cloned troops that spawn subtroops, such as Golems or Witches , will spawn these subtroops in a cloned state.
  * If more than one troop is inside the deployed Clone Spell, the troop spawned first will be cloned first.
  * For troops with death effects (such as Balloons or Ice Golems ), cloned troops that expire by overstepping their time limit will not exhibit any effects on death.
  * Cloning a Golem / Lava Hound at low health will create a new Golem/Lava Hound with full health. Use this to your advantage, as the cloned troop will be able to tank lots of damage, and for Lava Hounds, it will spawn more Lava Pups, which can distract the enemy and deal massive damage.
  * As the Clone Spell spawns clones randomly within its radius, you can place a Clone Spell partly over a layer of Walls to get some cloned troops behind them. This can be useful if you do not have Wall Breakers , Jump Spells or any other means of easily getting past the Walls. However, this should only be used in a pinch and if you do not have other solutions or if you are desparate.
  * Additionally, you could bring Balloons along with it to clone them instead. Since Balloons should be deployed first, the Balloons, as well as their clones, will tank for the Electro Dragon, so that the former will destroy defenses around the core and protect the latter, especially if there are Seeking Air Mines involved.
  * Clone Spells can be used similarly to clone other Clan Castle complements, such as Super Wizards or Super Archers cloaked under Invisibility Spells . The additional clones can greatly amplify the power of such squads.
  * If a cloned troop with an effect on death expires by lasting its maximum duration, it does not inflict damage or any other effects that would normally occur if it is destroyed. This is important to note if the cloned troop has a significant effect on death (such as cloned Ice Golems or Super Valkyries ), as these beneficial effects will not occur then.
  * The behavior of the Clone Spell on Super Troops with temporary abilities is not consistent and differs between different Super Troops : Cloned Super Barbarians will not have their ability active when created, even if the original still has its ability active. Cloned Rocket Balloons and Sneaky Goblins will spawn with their respective abilities at full duration, even if the haste or cloak has already expired on the troop being cloned. Cloned Super Minions will spawn with all of its long shots available, even if the original Super Minion has already used some or all of its long shots.
  * Cloned Rocket Balloons and Sneaky Goblins will spawn with their respective abilities at full duration, even if the haste or cloak has already expired on the troop being cloned.
  * Cloned Super Minions will spawn with all of its long shots available, even if the original Super Minion has already used some or all of its long shots.
  * You can also clone troops in the core of the base, as the cloned troops will do extra damage, and the extra troops will be effective at overwhelming your opponent's defenses, and they will disappear after most or even all of the core defenses have gone down.
  * Cloned units only last for a limited time. Use Tornado Traps to stall them and limit their effectiveness. Multiple Wall layers can be useful too, unless if they can jump over, mine under, or blow them up by either walking or dying (in the case of Wall Breakers ).
  * The Clone Spell multiplies the troop with the lowest housing space, which is commonly low-hitpoint troops like the Miner , the Balloon , the Hog Rider , the Bowler , the Giant or the Wizard . Use splash damage defenses and protect them so they can help destroy the cloned troops.

## Invisibility Spell  (rev 623168)
- info: Effect Radius: 4 tiles; Housing Space: 1; Spell Factory Level Required: 6
- stats: Level | Duration | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    1 | 3.5s | N/A | N/A | N/A
    2 | 3.75s | 5,000,000 | 3d | 9
    3 | 4s | 6,000,000 | 4d | 10
    4 | 4.25s | 7,000,000 | 5d | 11
  * "Invisibility Spell makes EVERYTHING except for Walls and Siege Machines invisible and untargetable within its radius."
  * The Invisibility Spell is the seventh Elixir Spell available for Town Hall 11, unlocked by upgrading the Spell Factory to level 6. It allows any offensive or defensive unit (with the exception of Walls and Siege Machines ) in its radius to turn invisible and be unable to be targeted by anything until they step out of the radius or the duration runs out. If the spell hits a building that the Troops were originally targeting, they will leave their previous target and engage to another one that's close to them.
  * If the spell hits a building that the Troops were originally targeting, they will leave their previous target and engage to another one that's close to them.
  * Invisible units can still be affected by other spells and can also be hit indirectly through splash-damaging attacks or otherwise.
  * Damaging projectiles will still hit any troops they targeted if they are sent before the spell has affected the targeted troop.
  * The Invisibility Spell can be used to buy time for slow troops such as Electro Dragons or Balloons under high DPS areas in order to survive longer.
  * It can be preferred over the Freeze Spell since instead of stopping one area from attacking any troops , it protects one group of troops from being attacked at all.
  * This is also useful for funnelling the Royal Champion , as the player can use the Invisibility Spell on the Royal Champion and some other defense such as Mortar , Cannon or Archer Tower and lead her to a more valuable defense, such as Inferno Tower , X-Bow or a Multi-Gear Tower .
  * The Invisibility Spell synergizes very well with the Electro Dragon. It can protect the Electro Dragon, make it skip less important buildings, and also allow it to chain back to said less important buildings before targeting them again.
  * Making a defense invisible will not prevent it from firing at any visible targets in its range. However, doing so may still be useful if only to guide defense-targeting troops to attack a more valuable defense.
  * Once the Eagle Artillery has locked on, it will fire, even if its targets later become invisible. Be sure to place your spell before it locks on or else it will be a waste.
  * Attacking Troops will always see each other. As such, Healers will heal a troop even after it becomes invisible.
  * Since Walls are not made invisible, a Jump Spell will be necessary to do this if the Clan Castle is surrounded by Walls, as the Skeleton Spell will not be able to spawn its Skeletons easily inside the Clan Castle compartment without making the Clan Castle invisible as well.
  * Clone Spells can be added to increase the number of troops spawned, amplifying the damage of the strategy.
  * Super Archers can be used to a similar effect, since their attacks can pierce through buildings from a very long range. If the player has problems in using Super Archer Blimps, the Throwers can also be used to do a similar thing, except they only deal damage to a target but has much more hitpoints, allowing them to survive Giant Bombs .
  * If the player has problems in using Super Archer Blimps, the Throwers can also be used to do a similar thing, except they only deal damage to a target but has much more hitpoints, allowing them to survive Giant Bombs .
  * Sometimes, a player can use an Invisibility Spell paired with about 8 Sneaky Goblins to take out a Town Hall . This works well when trying to snipe a Town Hall, especially at Town Halls 12 and above so its weapon or bomb at the end does not take out any offensive units. Care must be taken not to make the Town Hall invisible, as it will prevent the goblins from targeting it.
  * Traps are not affected by this spell. An invisible troop walking over a trap will trigger as normal. This also applies to Hidden Teslas . Buildings such as the Giga Tesla , Giga Inferno and Bomb Tower 's death bombs will still do damage to troops after being destroyed.

## Recall Spell  (rev 625472)
- info: Radius: 5 tiles; Targets: Ground & Air; Housing Space: 2; Spell Factory Level Required: 7
- stats: Level | Recalled Capacity | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    4 | 101 | 9,000,000 | 8d | 13
    5 | 107 | 13,000,000 | 9d | 14
    6 | 113 | 19,000,000 | 11d | 15
    7 | 120 | 29,000,000 | 16d | 16
  * " Attack going badly? Need to come in from another direction? The Recall Spell summons back Troops , Heroes and Pets to the deployment bar. You can then send the recalled units back out for a second strike."
  * Note that the Hero priority does not extend to Pets, which may be separated from the Hero if other high-housing space troops are recalled.
  * The Recall Spell can be used to forcibly call back units from the Town Hall weapon's explosion upon its destruction. This can save lower-health units from being otherwise obliterated, and can prevent significant HP loss on Heroes , and is especially useful against Town Halls 14 and above, since the Giga Inferno Poison Bomb's and the Inferno Artillery 's damage over time effects can prove devastating.
  * If two Heroes are being used for the same walk, both Heroes and their Pets will be prioritized, preventing any Healers from being recalled if both Heroes have Pets other than the Phoenix. If the Phoenix is assigned to one of them, with the other having a Pet, a level 2 Recall Spell is required to recall any Healers at all, with level 4 allowing the recalling of two Healers.
  * Having spawn holes (3x3 or larger areas that are completely empty, allowing attacking troops to be spawned) in the middle of a base will enable attackers to deploy troops with some sort of safety net (usually one or more Invisibility Spells to prevent being targeted) before using a Recall Spell to carry them to safety. This can be easily avoided by ensuring that no such holes exist in the base, keeping in mind that Traps and Hidden Teslas do not create their own no-spawn zones.
  * To prevent a Recall Spell from enabling Hero walks on multiple sides, you can try to defend against the Hero walk itself. For example, ground-mode X-Bows can create long-range pressure which may have to be responded to by a Rage Spell , while single-target Inferno Towers or Monoliths will force attackers to use a Freeze Spell or Invisibility Spell. Clan Castle troop complements built to shut down or stall Hero walks can render the strategy ineffective as well.

## Revive Spell  (rev 625258)
- info: Trigger Radius: 8 tiles; Targets: Heroes (Single Target); Housing Space: 2; Spell Factory Level Required: 8
- info: Sound Effect: https://clashofclans.fandom.com/wiki/File:Generic_SpellSelect.ogg; Captions: Spell chosen; Description: When the Revive Spell is selected from the deployment bar
- stats: Level | Hero Heal % | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    2 | 65% | 18,000,000 | 7d | 13
    3 | 70% | 19,000,000 | 8d | 14
    4 | 75% | 20,000,000 | 11d 12h | 15
    5 | 80% | 29,500,000 | 16d | 16
  * "The Healer's own special recipe! The Revive Spell brings a defeated Hero in its range back into the fight with some health. Bring this Spell into battle, and a floating golden heart will appear above any defeated Hero that can be revived. Upgrade the Revive Spell to increase the amount of health Heroes regain."
  * If there are no eligible Heroes in range if selected, the spell will disappear without taking effect, wasting the spell.
  * If there are multiple Heroes in range, the effect will only apply to the closest Hero to where the Spell was deployed.
  * If "Activate Hero Abilities on KO" is turned on in the settings, the Hero ability will be activated first before the Hero can be knocked out to be revived by the Revive Spell.
  * If the Hero is knocked out during a timed ability, such as that provided by the Rage Vial or Invisibility Vial , the ability timer will tick down while the Hero is knocked out. The ability can be resumed if the Hero is revived while the timer is ongoing.
  * If the Hero is knocked out while paired to a Pet , the Pet is considered to have decoupled from that Hero upon the Hero's initial KO. They will not rejoin the Hero (nor will the Hero influence their behavior) when the Hero is revived. In the case of the Dragon Duke, this means that his Pet, if it was an aerial Pet, will disable his Royal Rampage if he gets knocked out and revived.
  * In the case of the Dragon Duke, this means that his Pet, if it was an aerial Pet, will disable his Royal Rampage if he gets knocked out and revived.
  * To further improve this revival, the Phoenix must activate her ability first before you revive him when the Dragon Duke is knocked out after the invincibility. Beware that if the Phoenix isn't defeated after her invincibility ends, she may disable his Royal Rampage.
  * The Barbarian King is also an excellent choice, as he also has a lot of hitpoints. Additionally, if an ability is still active, such as the Giant Gauntlet , Stick Horse , or Rage Vial , the Revive Spell can allow the King to continue wrecking havoc. However, the King cannot jump over Walls, so his usefulness may be situational.
  * However, the King cannot jump over Walls, so his usefulness may be situational.
  * The Royal Champion can also be beneficial, as she can jump over Walls whilst the Barbarian King and the Archer Queen cannot.
  * If the Grand Warden wields the Fireball in a Warden Charge while being grabbed by the Hook Tower, you can let the Warden get knocked out in an area. Afterwards, turn the defeated Hero invisible and revive them before activating their ability and recalling them. Paired with Earthquake Spells , this can prove to be a deadly combo if used correctly.
  * Defending Heroes, Skeletons from Skeleton Traps and Clan Castle troops can distract the revived Hero while defenses deal damage.

## Totem Spell  (rev 625235)
- info: Radius: 5 tiles; Hitpoint Decay per Second: 334; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Ground & Air; Spell Factory Level Required: 9
- stats: Level | Totem Hitpoints | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    1 | 10,000 | N/A | N/A | N/A
    2 | 11,000 | 21,000,000 | 12d | 14
    3 | 12,000 | 22,000,000 | 14d | 15
    4 | 13,000 | 23,000,000 | 16d | 16
  * The Totem Spell summons a Totem with very high hitpoints. When deployed, the totem crashes down onto the deployment location, briefly stunning buildings and troops in a large radius. The stun causes nearby defenders to retarget. The retargeting does not force defenses to target the Totem. Defenses can attack a different target, if that unit is closer to the defense than the Totem is.
  * The retargeting does not force defenses to target the Totem. Defenses can attack a different target, if that unit is closer to the defense than the Totem is.
  * The Totem is considered both a ground and air troop for the purposes of being targeted by defenses; it can be hit by both ground-only and air-only defenses (in addition to defenses that can target both). The Totem is not considered an air troop for the purposes of determining if the Baby Dragon or Dragon Duke enrages or not; in other words, these units can trigger their enraged state near a Totem.
  * The Totem is not considered an air troop for the purposes of determining if the Baby Dragon or Dragon Duke enrages or not; in other words, these units can trigger their enraged state near a Totem.
  * The Totem is unable to trigger Traps or Clan Castle troops, nor will it trigger Spell Towers . The Totem also is unable to be affected by Clone Spells , Recall Spells , Ice Block Spells , or the auras of Life Gem or Apprentice Wardens . Although the Totem can be healed with a Healing Spell , it will not be targeted by Healers or Druids . The Totem can still be affected by Traps if other troops trigger them. However, the Totem is immune to displacement effects (e.g. from the Tornado Trap ).
  * The Totem can still be affected by Traps if other troops trigger them. However, the Totem is immune to displacement effects (e.g. from the Tornado Trap ).
  * The Totem is considered to take up 20 housing space. This is used when interacting with the Dark Crown , and is sufficient to withstand being ejected immediately by a Spring Trap (note that it can only be affected as such if other troops trigger the Trap). However, for the purposes of Eagle Artillery activation, the Totem Spell only counts as 5 housing space, the same as any 1-housing space spell.
  * Totem Spells are useful for distracting defending units to the summoned Totem, preventing important units like the Grand Warden in a Warden Charge or a Flame Flinger creating a funnel for the main army from being defeated prematurely. As one of the most used spells in most strategies at higher Town Hall levels and only requiring 1 spell housing space, they allow the attacking army to deal with the defending force and obliterate the base, provided that the attack is executed successfully.
  * However, since it stuns, it does not always redirect to the Totem, and will instead target the nearest unit if the defense's target is closer than the Totem, and can become worse if the defense deals splash damage, like Mortars and Scattershots , since small troops would get wiped out easily, and multi-targeting defenses like Multi-Archer Towers and multi-targeting Inferno Towers can still fire over troops.
  * On the other hand, Freeze Spells work better as it stops defending targets within its range, including Heroes, troops and buildings alike.
  * Totems have very high hitpoints, and are very useful for taking heavy fire in just one housing space, acting as both ground and air units that allows all defenses (including those that target air) to attack them. However, Totems are prone to single-target damage, so defenses like single-target Inferno Towers, Monoliths and fully powered Revenge Towers can easily take them out. In addition, they also have a limited time, which can be shortened if they take any form of damage, and will be destroyed if their hitpoints are depleted, and cannot trigger traps (including the Giga Bomb ).
  * In addition, they also have a limited time, which can be shortened if they take any form of damage, and will be destroyed if their hitpoints are depleted, and cannot trigger traps (including the Giga Bomb ).
  * The Eternal Tome cannot make the Totem immune to damage for the entire duration, because it is considered to be a stationary troop that acts like a building, unlike immovable Siege Machines and Furnaces .
  * When any units equal to or less than 18 housing space trigger the Spring Trap while the Totem is active, the Totem takes the damage instead of the troop, otherwise it would target them instead. This can be useful for Headhunters who are traveling to a defending Hero, allowing them to survive and eventually take them out.
  * Totems can lure ground troops to the Totem, especially in air attacks where the defending troops (like the Ice Golem or Skeletons that spawn from Witches) which could otherwise potentially make the difference between a 99% two-star time fail and a triple.
  * Totem Spells are particularly annoying for defending, especially due to its ability to stun everything in a large area.
  * However, since the Totem is spawned behind where the attacking force is, defending units and buildings must try their best to destroy the Totems as fast as possible, otherwise they may be destroyed easily, depending on the attacker's strategy.
  * Similarly, defending Heroes like the Archer Queen can take down Totems in several seconds due to her massive damage output, although the main attacking force can have problems, especially if they are entering the base.

## Poison Spell  (rev 624974)
- info: Radius: 4 tiles; Spell Duration: 16 Seconds; Damage Type: Area Splash; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Ground & Air; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 1
- stats: Level | Max damage per second | Speed Decrease | Attack Rate Decrease | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    9 | 320 | 48% | 70% | 135,000 | 7d | 13
    10 | 340 | 50% | 72% | 175,000 | 8d | 14
    11 | 360 | 51% | 73% | 230,000 | 9d 16h | 15
    12 | 380 | 52% | 74% | 350,000 | 14d 12h | 16
  * The Poison Spell is the first Dark Spell unlocked in the Dark Spell Factory and is automatically unlocked once the Dark Spell Factory has finished being built, requiring the Town Hall to be at level 8. It slows down all enemy Clan Castle Troops , Heroes , and Skeletons inside its area of effect (and causes them to take damage over time). However, buildings remain unaffected by Poison Spells.
  * The Poison Spell slowly ramps up in damage the longer a unit stays poisoned. The effect lasts for an additional 6 seconds after the unit leaves the poison radius.
  * A Poison Spell only takes one spell housing space, and deals more total damage over the course of its duration than the Lightning Spell , the other spell that can be used to deal with Clan Castle Troops, which takes up the same amount of spell capacity. This makes the Poison Spell very appealing as a way to deal with Clan Castle Troops along with a few troops to keep the enemy troops inside the area of effect of the Poison Spell.
  * A Poison Spell has a circular area of effect for scaling damage, whereas a Lightning Spell strikes a small radius, dealing instantaneous damage. Making effectiveness of both spells depend on the defending base's layout, as well as what and where the Clan Castle Troops are, and the player's army composition.
  * As of September 18, 2015, Poison Spells do scaling damage in a similar manner to a single-target Inferno Tower , just to a lesser extent. This scaling effect dramatically affects high-hitpoint units but deals much less damage against Heroes for balancing reasons. The Poison Spell is still useful to slow defending Heroes down; however, a Freeze Spell can also be a good alternative, which can also affect defenses around them. It can also be used to slow the movement and attack speed of the opposing heroes down.
  * The Poison Spell is still useful to slow defending Heroes down; however, a Freeze Spell can also be a good alternative, which can also affect defenses around them. It can also be used to slow the movement and attack speed of the opposing heroes down.
  * It can also be used to slow the movement and attack speed of the opposing heroes down.
  * If you see a Dragon , Baby Dragon , Balloons , or Valkyries on defense, it is good to use a Poison Spell on them, as the scaling effect is deadly against high-hitpoint units. It also decreases their speed, which makes them slower to reach the target. Although a single Poison Spell will not fully wipe off very high hitpoint troops like Dragons, Electro Dragons , Golems , and P.E.K.K.As , its slowing effect makes them highly vulnerable to other troops while active.
  * Although a single Poison Spell will not fully wipe off very high hitpoint troops like Dragons, Electro Dragons , Golems , and P.E.K.K.As , its slowing effect makes them highly vulnerable to other troops while active.
  * Multiple stacked Poisons Spells will speed up the scaling process but will not increase the maximum damage per second, so typically it is a waste if you place many at once.
  * While not a popular strategy, Poison Spells can be used to stack damage, making it possible to take out any Clan Castle units with enough spells.
  * As of the March 2016 update, defending troops will automatically attempt to escape the Poison Spell's AOE if they are currently not targeting any troop, in which case they will ignore the spell entirely. Healers and Heroes are not affected, as Healers cannot attack enemy troops and defending Heroes have a limited targeting range already. However, it is currently unknown whether the Druids will run away from the Poison Spell, but the Bears will always attempt to escape from it.
  * A good offensive strategy is to lure the Clan Castle troops to the corner and then attack them with high-damage troops like the Wizard or a high number of Archers . Usually, when the Poison Spell is dropped on any defending troops, and if they do not have any target, then the defending troops will walk out of the radius. So, it is best to attack them with troops while the defending troops are inside the Poison Spell to get its full potential. Alternatively, you can use a Freeze Spell to make the Clan Castle troops unable to move, allowing the Poison Spell to ramp up in damage.
  * Alternatively, you can use a Freeze Spell to make the Clan Castle troops unable to move, allowing the Poison Spell to ramp up in damage.
  * As the Loading Screen Hints would say, it is a very effective answer to Skeletons spawned by Skeleton Traps . You can use another expendable troop to activate them, then deploy your Poison Spell to wipe them out.
  * Use high-damage troops like the Baby Dragon or Valkyrie to still deal good damage while under the effect. If you can get one, an Inferno Dragon is your best bet since even under poison, the 3rd stage damage is extremely high. Troops with a high attack rate will be affected much because the spell does massive change to this stat.
  * More than one Poison Spell is normally not good. If you have a good idea of where an attacker may start a Queen or Warden Walk, then place a Hero such that their trigger radius is at or behind the Clan Castle trigger radius in that direction, so an attacker will have to fight a hero and the Clan Castle troops at the same time, potentially forcing more Poison Spells to be brought.

## Earthquake Spell  (rev 622962)
- info: Damage Type: Area Splash; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Buildings & Walls; Favorite Target: Walls; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 2
- stats: Level | Building Damage % | Troop Damage % | Radius | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    5 | 29% | N/A | 4.7 tiles | 42,000 | 3d 12h | 9
    6 | 29% | 5% | 4.7 tiles | 120,000 | 8d | 12
    7 | 29% | 10% | 4.7 tiles | 200,000 | 9d | 14
    8 | 29% | 14.5%* | 4.7 tiles | 330,000 | 13d 12h | 16
  * "Weaken Walls and buildings with crippling earthquakes! Earthquake Spells damage structures based on their maximum hitpoints. Repeated Earthquakes deal decreasing damage to the same buildings, but increasing damage to the same Walls. No Wall can withstand the might of four Earthquake Spells!"
  * Like the Lightning Spell , Earthquake Spells cannot damage resource storages, but can damage resource collectors. However, Earthquake Spells can also damage the Town Hall and Clan Castle . This is likely because while those buildings do store resources and can be looted from, they do not leak resources when damaged like proper Storages do, instead releasing all available loot upon destruction, and Earthquakes dealing diminishing damage with repeated usage means they cannot take out the Clan Castle or Town Hall by themselves.
  * This pattern continues regardless of the level of Earthquake Spells, so if you have multiple Earthquake Spells with different levels (which is possible through Clan Castle reinforcements), the damage done will be different depending on the order you drop them in. To maximize damage, one should drop their highest-level Earthquake Spells first.
  * Troops that have been hit by Earthquake Spells will take reduced damage from subsequent Earthquake Spells, even if they have moved away from the area-of-effect of previous Earthquake Spells. However, Earthquake Spells below level 6 (that do not damage them) are not counted when determining damage scaling, so the first Earthquake Spell that damages them will do full damage.
  * The Earthquake Boots ability counts as one Earthquake use, so using it will reduce the damage of future Earthquake Spells against the same target. The inverse is true: Earthquake Boots will do less damage against targets already affected by Earthquake Spells.
  * The Earthquake's damage formula is slightly different on Walls: the n {\displaystyle n} th quake will do 1 / n {\displaystyle 1/n} as much damage, but every quake after the first also does additional damage equal to 5 ( n − 1 ) 2 {\displaystyle 5(n-1)^2} % of the Wall's maximum health (i.e. 5, 20 and 45% extra for the second through fourth Earthquake Spells respectively).
  * Against buildings with sufficiently high hitpoints, an Earthquake Spell will do more damage than a Lightning Spell . Thus, one Earthquake Spell is often used with a complement of Lightning Spells if one wishes to destroy high-hitpoint buildings. This is commonly referred to as Zapquake. The best example is that a max level Earthquake Spell can replace 3 max level Lightning Spells in the case of a max level Eagle Artillery .
  * You cannot destroy any building using only Earthquake Spells. The damage scaling (even at max level) means that a full complement of Earthquake Spells will only be able to do approximately two-thirds damage at most.
  * They are great to use as an alternative to Wall Breakers . As Earthquake Spells deal increasing damage to Walls , they are much more effective than using its counterpart, Lightning Spell, against Walls. They are therefore very useful for ground attacks if Walls might get in the way.
  * Unlike the Lightning Spell, Earthquake Spells can damage Town Halls and Clan Castles. Both have high health, and Clan Castle being taken out prematurely can prevent Clan Castle troops from spawning. Versus Town Halls of level 12 up to 16, if using defense-targeting troops, consider purposely triggering the Town Hall's defense by using an Earthquake spell so these troops will begin to target the Town Hall.
  * Versus Town Halls of level 12 up to 16, if using defense-targeting troops, consider purposely triggering the Town Hall's defense by using an Earthquake spell so these troops will begin to target the Town Hall.
  * A single Earthquake Spell can be used to weaken buildings sufficiently for direct-damage Hero abilities to finish them off. A particularly notable use case for this is with the Fireball , which can benefit greatly as the extra damage added by the Earthquake can be enough to destroy key defenses like the Scattershot or Monolith .
  * Against buildings that are close to a weaponized Builder's Hut, note that the Builder will be able to slowly repair all affected buildings in the radius. It is thus not advised to use four Earthquake Spells to open a compartment and/or damage the target buildings until your troops are nearing the compartment to be opened, to minimize the effects of the defending Builders. However, do not leave it too late either, as not doing so in time might mean your troops may be unnecessarily stuck on Walls or move away from the unopened compartments.
  * On the flipside, it could be advisable to instead use a Jump Spell to bypass a single compartment of Walls to optimize spell capacity in your army. Using 4 Earthquake Spells for a single compartment of Walls would often be inefficient and unnecessary in most cases.
  * Against opponents with Town Hall 14 or higher, Builder's Huts should be watched out for if attempting a Zapquake attack, as the Builders can repair buildings while the spells are doing damage. To prevent the Zapquake from failing, one should drop all of the spells involved as quickly as possible. It is advised to also bring more damage than is needed to destroy the target defenses, such that the Builder's repair fails to save the target defenses.
  * The player should never use Earthquake Spells on buildings affected by an Overgrowth Spell , as the damage would be completely wasted.
  * Using the Fireball alongside the Earthquake Spell will destroy a huge area of the base, provided that if both of them are sufficiently leveled to destroy main defenses. Otherwise, they will be severely damaged, and said defenses will eventually take out most of your army, if not all. Using the second Earthquake Spell can also allow it to increase the chances of destroying major defenses.
  * Otherwise, they will be severely damaged, and said defenses will eventually take out most of your army, if not all.

## Haste Spell  (rev 620321)
- info: Effect Type: Area Splash; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Ground & Air; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 3
- stats: Level | Speed Increase | Spell Duration | Radius | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    4 | 46 | 25 seconds | 4 tiles | 30,000 | 3d | 8
    5 | 52 | 30 seconds | 4 tiles | 38,500 | 4d | 9
    6 | 56 | 30 seconds | 4 tiles | 200,000 | 9d | 15
    7 | 56 | 30 seconds | 5 tiles | 320,000 | 12d 12h | 16
  * "Put some hustle in even your heaviest units ! Haste Spells lack the damage boost of Rage Spells , but provide an even better boost to movement speed. A lower storage space also allows you to take more of them into battle !"
  * The Haste Spell is the 3rd Dark Elixir Spell unlocked at Dark Spell Factory level 3 that requires the Town Hall to be at level 9. Similar to a Rage Spell , it boosts the movement speed of troops in its area of effect, but to a greater effect; however, the Haste Spell does not increase their damage. The Haste Spell is half the housing space and also has a slightly smaller radius than the Rage Spell.
  * Like with Healing Spells and Rage Spells, the Haste Spell has a reduced effect on Heroes , only granting 50% of its speed boost to them.
  * Haste Spells may be used to counteract the effect of Air Sweepers . Deploy the spell on slow air troops such as Electro Dragons and Balloons , and it will counteract the effect of the Air Sweeper by putting them back into where they were faster. However, if you can destroy Air Sweepers, it is better to destroy them directly, as it is not the most consistent.
  * However, if you can destroy Air Sweepers, it is better to destroy them directly, as it is not the most consistent.
  * The Haste Spell is most effective in aiding slower but more powerful troops, such as Balloons , Electro Dragons , Root Riders , Golems and Ice Golems . It is also effective on troops with abilities that last only temporarily, such as the Rocket Balloon , so that they can make use of the ability against their targets for longer. However, troops with fast movement speed would not benefit much from this, as there is no damage increase. It also does not boost attack speed, unlike the Haste Vial .
  * However, troops with fast movement speed would not benefit much from this, as there is no damage increase. It also does not boost attack speed, unlike the Haste Vial .
  * This spell, when paired with the Invisibility Spell or Overgrowth Spell , can help to facilitate funneling, since the Haste guides the troops to the funneled area faster.
  * In Zap attacks, where the attacker brings lots of Lightning Spells , the Haste Spell is very important if the troops are slow but strong since the spell is much more efficient at moving troops than other spells.
  * You can also use the Haste Spell at the last seconds of the battle in which your slow troops are away of non-defensive buildings like a Builder's Hut , so that they are able to destroy it and get 3 stars. You can also drop the spell before the Giga Bomb explodes, avoiding the death damage, especially if the bomb leaves poison behind.
  * You can also drop the spell before the Giga Bomb explodes, avoiding the death damage, especially if the bomb leaves poison behind.

## Skeleton Spell  (rev 623005)
- info: Radius: 3.5 tiles; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Ground; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 4
- info: Preferred Target: None; Attack Type: Melee (Ground Only); Movement Speed: 24; Attack Speed: 1s; Range: 0.4 tiles; Damage per Second: 25; Hitpoints: 30; Armor Hitpoints: 30
- stats: Level | Skeletons generated | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    5 | 16 | 40,000 | 2d 12h | 10
    6 | 17 | 50,000 | 3d | 10
    7 | 18 | 75,000 | 4d | 11
    8 | 19 | 135,000 | 6d | 13
  * "Summon an army of Skeletons anywhere on the battlefield! Put a few extra swords when and where you need them most. Skeletons do not trigger traps ."
  * The Skeleton Spell is the 4th Dark Spell unlocked at level 4 Dark Spell Factory that requires the Town Hall to be at level 9. When casting this spell, it summons several Skeletons on the battlefield. These Skeletons are very weak, but can provide a good distraction.
  * Like Skeletons summoned by Witches , they do not trigger Traps or Clan Castle troops.
  * The spell's skeletons do much less damage to resource buildings. In particular, this affects its interaction against the Town Hall, preventing it from destroying it easily.
  * If you decided to leave the defending Archer Queen for something else during your opening phase, a good idea is to wait for the Balloons and Lava Hounds to come around, rage them and drop a Skeleton Spell. The spell will distract and take out the Queen and possible other buildings, and with the added bonus of raging your main army. However, beware of splash surrounding the Queen. If there is a Bomb Tower nearby, consider taking the Queen out during the opening phase.
  * In GoHo ( Golem + Hog Rider ) attacks, you may encounter Skeleton Traps which spawn a few Skeletons to follow and damage your Hog Riders. Dropping a single Skeleton Spell will help get rid of the Skeletons chipping away at your Hogs' health. Wait until your Hogs run into a Healing Spell , then drop your Skeleton Spell.
  * More generally, the Skeleton Spells are effective against compartments containing only single-target defenses and slow-firing defenses (which includes the Mortar and Scattershot ). They can be used to cover troops as they advance through this compartment; in particular, the Royal Champion on her own can be used in conjunction with Skeleton Spells in this manner to clear such a compartment without taking significant damage.
  * Skeleton Spells can be used to destroy unguarded defenses with dead zones like the Mortar or Eagle Artillery . At 1 spell slot, this can be very valuable to any attacker. However, defenses beside this might spoil the strategy, to try to distract the defenses surrounding it and then place skeleton spells. However, the poison spell tower will counter this, because it will take 2 ticks to kill the skeleton (1 for shield,1 for the skeleton themselves).

## Bat Spell  (rev 623006)
- info: Radius: 3.5 tiles; Housing Space: 1; Targets: Defenses; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 5
- info: Preferred Target: Defenses; Attack Type: Air; Movement Speed: 56; Attack Speed: 2s; Range: 0.8 Tiles; Damage per Second: 30; Damage vs. Rescources: x0.4; Hitpoints: 20
- stats: Level | Bats generated | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    5 | 21 | 47,500 | 4d | 10
    6 | 22 | 140,000 | 7d | 13
    7 | 23 | 220,000 | 8d 12h | 15
    8 | 25 | 300,000 | 13d | 16
  * The Bat Spell is the 5th Dark Spell unlocked at level 5 Dark Spell Factory that requires the Town Hall to be at level 10. When casting this spell, it summons several Bats on the battlefield over time (its method of spawning is similar to a Skeleton Spell ). These Bats are low on health and target defenses.
  * Like Skeletons , Bats do not trigger traps or Clan Castle troops .
  * Bats spawned from the Bat Spell deal less damage to resource storages; in particular, this applies to the Town Hall's Giga Tesla , and Giga Inferno , as it is classified as both a defense and a resource storage.
  * The Bats also relate to the Builder Base troop also known as the Night Witch . The Night Witch spawns Bats that relate to the Bats in the Bat Spell. However, the Night Witch's Bats attack any target.
  * Bats can destroy most single-target exposed defenses relatively easily.
  * Bats can be very effective in distracting single-target Inferno Towers from targeting your tanks ( Golems , Lava Hounds, etc.). But, against defenses which target multiple targets such as multi-target Inferno Towers, the Bats will get killed quickly.
  * Wizard Towers are excellent in countering Bat-based attacks. Consider their placements carefully when planning such an attack. Either distract them with another troop like a Giant or Ice Golem or use a Freeze Spell to stop them.
  * It can be viable to use Bat Spells in masses with Lava Hounds. The Lava Hounds will be able to tank well for the Bats (as well as shield them from Inferno Towers) while the Bats do damage. However, if the Lava Hound gets targeted by an Air Bomb, the resulting explosion from the traps will destroy the bats if nearby said troop afterwards.
  * However, if the Lava Hound gets targeted by an Air Bomb, the resulting explosion from the traps will destroy the bats if nearby said troop afterwards.
  * If defenses are targeting your tanks, place a few Bat Spells on the defenses to destroy them. However, Make sure that there isn't much defenses that are free, since they should be able to take out the bats easily.
  * A good strategy for Bat Spells would be to place a lot of them down near the center of the base, along with a Rage Spell and a Freeze Spell, aiming to destroy single-target Inferno Towers, Air Defenses, and the Eagle Artillery at the beginning of an attack. Anything else would be a bonus. Then use the rest of your attack to maul the rest of the base. The Battle Blimp used with the Super Wizards paired with Invisibility Spells (Blizzard) or the Electrone (Balloons and Electro Dragons with Clone and Rage) strategy is still more effective at decimating the core.
  * With a similar thought process, use something else that does not require more than one or two Rage Spells to take out a portion of the base, along with the Town Hall and Splash defenses to create a clear path and make the job much easier for the Bats to breeze through the rest of the base.
  * Another strategy is to use a horde of bats to take down a whole area of single-targeting defenses. The Invisibility Spell will be useful for them, as well as the Freeze Spell to freeze splash defenses as necessary.
  * However, if it and other defenses are too close to each other, especially to those which target ground and air units and doing multi-target, fast attacking or splash damage, a Freeze Spell will be able to stop them from attacking altogether, and the Bats may will be able to destroy these defenses before the Freeze Spell expires.
  * If multi-target Inferno Towers are unavailable (due to being upgraded, for example), rapid-firing defenses such as X-Bows set to ground and air mode can also destroy Bats quickly. Archer Towers set to fast attack can also prove viable, as well as the Multi-Archer Tower .
  * Air Bombs can be helpful to sweep up Bats that are following closely behind other air troops, but as the Bats cannot trigger the Air Bomb themselves, it may not be a reliable option.
  * Resource storages, which also includes the Town Hall itself are a good way to soak up damage from the Bats, since they deal 15% of the damage when attacking these types of buildings, either causing the attacker to likely time fail, or to possibly get a 1-star, provided if the Town Hall is not destroyed.
  * The defending Electro Titan is able to wipe out Bats instantly due to her passive damaging aura, even if you apply the Invisibility Spell on them. Her aura can neither be stopped by Freeze Spells nor Poison Spells, so the only way to deal with the aura is to lure the Electro Titan away from the area you want to deploy your Bats.

## Overgrowth Spell  (rev 623007)
- info: Radius: 6 tiles; Effect Type: Area Splash; Targets: Ground & Air; Housing Space: 2; Dark Spell Factory Level Required: 6
- stats: Level | Duration | Research Cost | Research Time | Laboratory Level Required
    2 | 23 seconds | 62,500 | 5d | 10
